{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01GNttJ5yFHAETqh51kibx3F","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01JZzM2bHcL5mFmFTYiMViAF","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-13176","account":"root","command":"cat > /root/xling_calib_direction/code/probing_controls_v2.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"\nProbing control task v2: 5-fold CV evaluation to avoid training-set overfitting.\nUses exact same hyperparameters as mvp_001 probes (C=1.0, L2, lbfgs, max_iter=2000, StandardScaler).\n\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport glob\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\nfrom sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\n\nPROBE_CONFIG = dict(C=1.0, penalty=\"l2\", solver=\"lbfgs\", max_iter=2000)\nN_FOLDS = 5\nN_PERMUTATIONS = 10\n\ndef cv_auroc(X, y, seed=42):\n    \"\"\"5-fold CV AUROC with StandardScaler per fold, matching mvp_001 probe config.\"\"\"\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=N_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    fold_aurocs = []\n    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(X, y):\n        scaler = StandardScaler()\n        X_train = scaler.fit_transform(X[train_idx])\n        X_test = scaler.transform(X[test_idx])\n        clf = LogisticRegression(**PROBE_CONFIG, random_state=seed)\n        clf.fit(X_train, y[train_idx])\n        logits = clf.decision_function(X_test)\n        if len(np.unique(y[test_idx])) < 2:\n            continue\n        fold_aurocs.append(roc_auc_score(y[test_idx], logits))\n    return np.mean(fold_aurocs) if fold_aurocs else 0.5\n\ndef run_controls(activations_dir, seed=42):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    \n    en_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(activations_dir, \"layer*_en.pt\")))\n    layer_indices = [int(os.path.basename(f).split(\"_\")[0].replace(\"layer\", \"\")) for f in en_files]\n    \n    print(f\"Layers: {layer_indices}\")\n    print(f\"Config: C={PROBE_CONFIG['C']}, solver={PROBE_CONFIG['solver']}, \"\n          f\"max_iter={PROBE_CONFIG['max_iter']}, {N_FOLDS}-fold CV, {N_PERMUTATIONS} permutations\")\n    print(f\"\\n{'Layer':>5s} {'CV AUROC':>9s} {'Perm AUROC':>11s} {'Perm std':>9s} {'Selectivity':>12s}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 55)\n    \n    results = []\n    for li in layer_indices:\n        data = torch.load(os.path.join(activations_dir, f\"layer{li}_en.pt\"), weights_only=True)\n        X = data[\"activations\"].numpy()\n        y = data[\"labels\"].numpy()\n        \n        if len(np.unique(y)) < 2:\n            print(f\"{li:>5d} SKIP (single class)\")\n            continue\n        \n        # Real probe: 5-fold CV\n        real_auroc = cv_auroc(X, y, seed=seed)\n        \n        # Permuted probes: 5-fold CV with shuffled labels\n        perm_aurocs = []\n        for p in range(N_PERMUTATIONS):\n            y_perm = rng.permutation(y)\n            perm_auroc = cv_auroc(X, y_perm, seed=seed + p + 1)\n            perm_aurocs.append(perm_auroc)\n        \n        mean_perm = np.mean(perm_aurocs)\n        std_perm = np.std(perm_aurocs)\n        selectivity = real_auroc - mean_perm\n        \n        results.append({\n            \"layer\": li, \"real_auroc\": real_auroc, \"perm_auroc_mean\": mean_perm,\n            \"perm_auroc_std\": std_perm, \"selectivity\": selectivity\n        })\n        print(f\"{li:>5d} {real_auroc:>9.4f} {mean_perm:>11.4f} {std_perm:>9.4f} {selectivity:>+12.4f}\")\n    \n    # Save\n    out_path = os.path.join(activations_dir, \"..\", \"results\", \"probing_controls_v2.csv\")\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out_path), exist_ok=True)\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        f.write(\"layer,real_cv_auroc,perm_cv_auroc_mean,perm_cv_auroc_std,selectivity\\n\")\n        for r in results:\n            f.write(f\"{r['layer']},{r['real_auroc']:.6f},{r['perm_auroc_mean']:.6f},\"\n                    f\"{r['perm_auroc_std']:.6f},{r['selectivity']:.6f}\\n\")\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {out_path}\")\n    \n    print(f\"\\nSanity check: L27 real CV AUROC should be close to mvp_001's 0.7541 \"\n          f\"(80/20 split). Got: {[r for r in results if r['layer']==27][0]['real_auroc']:.4f}\" \n          if any(r['layer']==27 for r in results) else \"\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    run_controls(\"/root/xling_calib_direction/activations\")\nPYEOF\necho \"FILE_WRITTEN_OK\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":946,"cache_read_input_tokens":24889,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":946},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ce128b30-af95-46ba-96cb-2c3f43f84521","uuid":"2c9156ed-b0ea-49f9-affd-20907fbf2c60"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_0121CWKDDvrwsSD2RhpfYKGo","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"The warnings are printing for each cv_auroc call. So 13 lines means several CV runs have been completed. Each FutureWarning block is 3 lines. So from 7 to 13, we've had 2 more warnings = 2 more cv_auroc calls. But earlier we saw warnings increasing one by one (8, 9, 10, 11), so it seems like 1 warning per cv_auroc call, not 5.\n \nI'm reconsidering the warning deduplication logic. Python's default filter uses `(text, category, lineno)` as the key, so identical warnings should only appear once. But I'm seeing 15 warnings now when I expected fewer, which suggests either sklearn changed its behavior in v1.8+ or something's resetting the warning filter between calls. The StratifiedKFold loop creating 5 folds might be the culprit here. Each fold creates a new `LogisticRegression(**PROBE_CONFIG, ...)`. The `FutureWarning` is triggered in `fit()`. Python's warning filter uses `__warningregistry__` which is a module-level dict. The key includes the line number and the message text. Since all calls have the same message and line, only the first call within the module's lifetime shows the warning.\n\nBUT - if sklearn's `_logistic.py` is clearing `__warningregistry__` or using a different mechanism, we'd see more. In newer sklearn, they use `warnings.warn` which respects the default `once` filter... but the `default` filter actually shows it once per call location (source file + line number of the caller). Since all our calls come from the same location in `cv_auroc`, only one should be shown.\n\nThe warning behavior might be affected by how sklearn uses `stacklevel` parameters, but that's getting into the weeds. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01Wdujc6ojELM3hK8e3KwCNg","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01UoEd5NszdntmBT1ahimkSx","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-13176","account":"root","command":"cat > /root/xling_calib_direction/code/probing_controls_v2.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"\nProbing control task v2: 5-fold CV evaluation to avoid training-set overfitting.\nUses exact same hyperparameters as mvp_001 probes (C=1.0, L2, lbfgs, max_iter=2000, StandardScaler).\nParallelized across folds and permutations using joblib.\n\"\"\"\nimport os, glob, warnings\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\nfrom sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\nfrom joblib import Parallel, delayed\n\nwarnings.filterwarnings(\"ignore\", category=FutureWarning)\n\nPROBE_CONFIG = dict(C=1.0, penalty=\"l2\", solver=\"lbfgs\", max_iter=2000)\nN_FOLDS = 5\nN_PERMUTATIONS = 10\n\ndef _fit_one_fold(X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, seed):\n    scaler = StandardScaler()\n    Xtr = scaler.fit_transform(X_train)\n    Xte = scaler.transform(X_test)\n    clf = LogisticRegression(**PROBE_CONFIG, random_state=seed)\n    clf.fit(Xtr, y_train)\n    logits = clf.decision_function(Xte)\n    if len(np.unique(y_test)) < 2:\n        return None\n    return roc_auc_score(y_test, logits)\n\ndef cv_auroc(X, y, seed=42):\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=N_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    splits = list(skf.split(X, y))\n    results = Parallel(n_jobs=N_FOLDS)(\n        delayed(_fit_one_fold)(X[tr], y[tr], X[te], y[te], seed)\n        for tr, te in splits\n    )\n    valid = [r for r in results if r is not None]\n    return np.mean(valid) if valid else 0.5\n\ndef _run_one_perm(X, y, rng_seed, cv_seed):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(rng_seed)\n    y_perm = rng.permutation(y)\n    return cv_auroc(X, y_perm, seed=cv_seed)\n\ndef run_controls(activations_dir, seed=42):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    \n    en_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(activations_dir, \"layer*_en.pt\")))\n    layer_indices = [int(os.path.basename(f).split(\"_\")[0].replace(\"layer\", \"\")) for f in en_files]\n    \n    print(f\"Layers: {layer_indices}\")\n    print(f\"Config: C={PROBE_CONFIG['C']}, solver={PROBE_CONFIG['solver']}, \"\n          f\"max_iter={PROBE_CONFIG['max_iter']}, {N_FOLDS}-fold CV, {N_PERMUTATIONS} permutations\")\n    print(f\"\\n{'Layer':>5s} {'CV AUROC':>9s} {'Perm AUROC':>11s} {'Perm std':>9s} {'Selectivity':>12s}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 55)\n    \n    results = []\n    for li in layer_indices:\n        data = torch.load(os.path.join(activations_dir, f\"layer{li}_en.pt\"), weights_only=True)\n        X = data[\"activations\"].numpy()\n        y = data[\"labels\"].numpy()\n        \n        if len(np.unique(y)) < 2:\n            print(f\"{li:>5d} SKIP (single class)\")\n            continue\n        \n        real_auroc = cv_auroc(X, y, seed=seed)\n        \n        # Generate all permutation seeds upfront for reproducibility\n        perm_seeds = [int(rng.randint(0, 2**31)) for _ in range(N_PERMUTATIONS)]\n        cv_seeds = [seed + p + 1 for p in range(N_PERMUTATIONS)]\n        \n        # Run permutations in parallel (outer parallelism)\n        perm_aurocs = Parallel(n_jobs=min(N_PERMUTATIONS, 10))(\n            delayed(_run_one_perm)(X, y, ps, cs)\n            for ps, cs in zip(perm_seeds, cv_seeds)\n        )\n        \n        mean_perm = np.mean(perm_aurocs)\n        std_perm = np.std(perm_aurocs)\n        selectivity = real_auroc - mean_perm\n        \n        results.append({\n            \"layer\": li, \"real_auroc\": real_auroc, \"perm_auroc_mean\": mean_perm,\n            \"perm_auroc_std\": std_perm, \"selectivity\": selectivity\n        })\n        print(f\"{li:>5d} {real_auroc:>9.4f} {mean_perm:>11.4f} {std_perm:>9.4f} {selectivity:>+12.4f}\")\n    \n    out_path = os.path.join(activations_dir, \"..\", \"results\", \"probing_controls_v2.csv\")\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out_path), exist_ok=True)\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        f.write(\"layer,real_cv_auroc,perm_cv_auroc_mean,perm_cv_auroc_std,selectivity\\n\")\n        for r in results:\n            f.write(f\"{r['layer']},{r['real_auroc']:.6f},{r['perm_auroc_mean']:.6f},\"\n                    f\"{r['perm_auroc_std']:.6f},{r['selectivity']:.6f}\\n\")\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {out_path}\")\n    \n    if any(r['layer']==27 for r in results):\n        l27 = [r for r in results if r['layer']==27][0]\n        print(f\"\\nSanity check: L27 real CV AUROC should be close to mvp_001's 0.7541 \"\n              f\"(80/20 split). Got: {l27['real_auroc']:.4f}\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    run_controls(\"/root/xling_calib_direction/activations\")\nPYEOF\necho \"FILE_WRITTEN_OK\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":235,"cache_read_input_tokens":48782,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":235},"output_tokens":73,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ce128b30-af95-46ba-96cb-2c3f43f84521","uuid":"b0c8387e-b7a4-4968-85f8-77da87c9ca56"}

> /root/xling_calib_direction/code/probing_controls_v2.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"\nProbing control task v2: 5-fold CV evaluation to avoid training-set overfitting.\nUses exact same hyperparameters as mvp_001 probes (C=1.0, L2, lbfgs, max_iter=2000, StandardScaler).\nParallelized across permutations using joblib.\n\"\"\"\nimport os, glob, warnings\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\nfrom sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\nfrom joblib import Parallel, delayed\n\nwarnings.filterwarnings(\"ignore\", category=FutureWarning)\n\nPROBE_CONFIG = dict(C=1.0, penalty=\"l2\", solver=\"lbfgs\", max_iter=2000)\nN_FOLDS = 5\nN_PERMUTATIONS = 10\n\ndef cv_auroc(X, y, seed=42):\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=N_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    fold_aurocs = []\n    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(X, y):\n        scaler = StandardScaler()\n        X_train = scaler.fit_transform(X[train_idx])\n        X_test = scaler.transform(X[test_idx])\n        clf = LogisticRegression(**PROBE_CONFIG, random_state=seed)\n        clf.fit(X_train, y[train_idx])\n        logits = clf.decision_function(X_test)\n        if len(np.unique(y[test_idx])) < 2:\n            continue\n        fold_aurocs.append(roc_auc_score(y[test_idx], logits))\n    return np.mean(fold_aurocs) if fold_aurocs else 0.5\n\ndef _perm_auroc(X, y, perm_seed, cv_seed):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(perm_seed)\n    y_perm = rng.permutation(y)\n    return cv_auroc(X, y_perm, seed=cv_seed)\n\ndef run_controls(activations_dir, seed=42):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    \n    en_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(activations_dir, \"layer*_en.pt\")))\n    layer_indices = [int(os.path.basename(f).split(\"_\")[0].replace(\"layer\", \"\")) for f in en_files]\n    \n    print(f\"Layers: {layer_indices}\")\n    print(f\"Config: C={PROBE_CONFIG['C']}, solver={PROBE_CONFIG['solver']}, \"\n          f\"max_iter={PROBE_CONFIG['max_iter']}, {N_FOLDS}-fold CV, {N_PERMUTATIONS} permutations\")\n    print(f\"\\n{'Layer':>5s} {'CV AUROC':>9s} {'Perm AUROC':>11s} {'Perm std':>9s} {'Selectivity':>12s}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 55)\n    \n    results = []\n    for li in layer_indices:\n        data = torch.load(os.path.join(activations_dir, f\"layer{li}_en.pt\"), weights_only=True)\n        X = data[\"activations\"].numpy()\n        y = data[\"labels\"].numpy()\n        \n        if len(np.unique(y)) < 2:\n            print(f\"{li:>5d} SKIP (single class)\")\n            continue\n        \n        real_auroc = cv_auroc(X, y, seed=seed)\n        \n        perm_seeds = [int(rng.randint(0, 2**31)) for _ in range(N_PERMUTATIONS)]\n        cv_seeds = [seed + p + 1 for p in range(N_PERMUTATIONS)]\n        \n        perm_aurocs = Parallel(n_jobs=N_PERMUTATIONS, prefer=\"processes\")(\n            delayed(_perm_auroc)(X, y, ps, cs)\n            for ps, cs in zip(perm_seeds, cv_seeds)\n        )\n        \n        mean_perm = np.mean(perm_aurocs)\n        std_perm = np.std(perm_aurocs)\n        selectivity = real_auroc - mean_perm\n        \n        results.append({\n            \"layer\": li, \"real_auroc\": real_auroc, \"perm_auroc_mean\": mean_perm,\n            \"perm_auroc_std\": std_perm, \"selectivity\": selectivity\n        })\n        print(f\"{li:>5d} {real_auroc:>9.4f} {mean_perm:>11.4f} {std_perm:>9.4f} {selectivity:>+12.4f}\")\n    \n    out_path = os.path.join(activations_dir, \"..\", \"results\", \"probing_controls_v2.csv\")\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out_path), exist_ok=True)\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        f.write(\"layer,real_cv_auroc,perm_cv_auroc_mean,perm_cv_auroc_std,selectivity\\n\")\n        for r in results:\n            f.write(f\"{r['layer']},{r['real_auroc']:.6f},{r['perm_auroc_mean']:.6f},\"\n                    f\"{r['perm_auroc_std']:.6f},{r['selectivity']:.6f}\\n\")\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {out_path}\")\n    \n    if any(r['layer']==27 for r in results):\n        l27 = [r for r in results if r['layer']==27][0]\n        print(f\"\\nSanity check: L27 real CV AUROC should be close to mvp_001's 0.7541 \"\n              f\"(80/20 split). Got: {l27['real_auroc']:.4f}\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    run_controls(\"/root/xling_calib_direction/activations\")\nPYEOF\necho \"OK\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1887,"cache_read_input_tokens":49017,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1887},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ce128b30-af95-46ba-96cb-2c3f43f84521","uuid":"b00fb9d7-78e5-4615-a32c-39f8e2e8592d"}
